Instant.ofEpochMilli(num) : Instant.ofEpochSecond(num)\n        return instant.atZone(zone).toLocalDate()\n    }\n    return s.contains(\"T\") ? denominator is 0.\ndef pct = { numerator, denominator ->\n    if (numerator == null || denominator == null) return BigDecimal.ZERO\n    def d = (denominator as BigDecimal)\n    if (d == 0) return prior (increase), negative = current < prior (decrease).\n// Returns a numeric change percentage (BigDecimal, 2 decimals).\n// Positive = current > prior (increase), negative = current < prior (decrease).\n// Special case: if prior is 0 and current is positive, return 100 (interpreted as full increase from zero baseline).\ndef changePct = { current, prior ->\n    if (current == null ||    = (binding.hasVariable('topAgentCost')    && topAgentCost    instanceof List) ? topAgentCost    : []\ndef rawMonthAgents     = (binding.hasVariable('monthAgents')     && monthAgents     instanceof
